Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The newtonian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 448. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 332 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by newtonian generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 448, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 332. QED.
